← Atlas

Historical geometry audit

Audited all 197 dated frames and 1055 timeline territory entries across 14 chapters. Removed every use of the old modern-administrative province units. 0 entries have no drawable regional coverage and 0 represent archaeological sites; these are location labels, not filled political territories. 13 entries have dashed cultural-region reconstructions. Reconstructed outlines remain approximate. This is an exhaustive audit of the app's geometry provenance, not a claim that every historical frontier is now known.

Cliopatria v0.2.0 / Seshat · Research paper · CC BY 4.0 · Full methodology / Cross-era review · Machine-readable audit

Historical synthesis combines Bennett et al. with the individually cited scholarly and archaeological sources. Polygons are clipped to the atlas, rounded, and adjusted where cited. The outer viewing-window edges are not historical frontiers. A source year range is not evidence of an exact surveyed frontier. Location-only labels deliberately make no territorial claim.
